Intro
BAZIS-CNC module function
BAZIS-CNC module is a part of BAZIS system and it allows to create control programs for
panels processing on boring and milling CNC machines. The models can be saved in files of
3D models, fragments, sheets, libraries of BAZIS system. The module can be used as a stand-
alone application and run from BAZIS-Woodworker.
HELP use
Adobe Reader window control elements – a free program for PDF documents viewing, allows
to use different modes of document access.
Tabs tab contains a structured list of document sections.
Editing – Find command allows to find a text string in the current document. You can also
use <Ctrl>+<F> key combination for this purpose. For next string entry push <F3> button.
Editing – Search command allows to fulfill words advanced search.
On-line document contains hypertext links. They include Tab tab sections names, image and
table numbers in a text, document underlined sections links (fig. 1, a) or with section number
(fig. 1, b).
1.  
If you drag a cursor to a link, a cursor will change its form. To follow a link, click it with mouse
left button.
To return to the document place where you followed a link from, push <Alt>+f key combina-
tion, in this case use <left arrow>, but not a button which is also 4 number button on numeric
keyboard.
Conventions of description
Usually, the application command can be activat ed in different ways, e.g. in Main menu by
pushing the button, double click at field of context menu. One of the ways is described in User
Guide.
If the commands are in Main menu sections, it  is necessary to open sequentially the menu
sections for a concrete command activation. E.g. in order to create a new Model document,
it is necessary to fulfill the following actions:
▼ Open File menu.
▼ Call Open... command (fig. 2).
a) b)
Fig. 1.

[Página 14]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
14
2.  
This action sequence in Help system is described in the following way: call File – Open...
command. Module commands and operating system call is executed in the same way. If you
use a command button, this button image will be moved to paragraph left field. Keyboard key
names are in angle brackets and italicized. Key combinations are written in the following way.
<Key 1> + <Key 2>. This record means, that it is necessary to press <Key 1>, then holding
it, – <Key 2>.
<Key 1>, <Key 2>. This record means, that it is necessary to press <Key 1>, release it and
push <Key 2>.
Notes, hints and warnings are marked with the following icons:
Technical support
If there are some problems with BAZIS system applications installation and use, as well as
with protection keys, it is recommended to follow the actions.
▼ Use the system documentation and find there some information about troubleshooting.
▼ If there are no recommendations, consult the technical support of your supplier of BAZIS sys-
tem software products (regional dealer).

puter and peripheral equipment configurations. Indicate the serial number of protection key.

Chapter 1.Module general information
1.1. Module start
To start BAZIS-CNC, call Start – Programs – BAZIS – BAZIS-CNC command.
Choose machine control system dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 1.1).
1.1.  
Activate options of machines (postprocessors) control systems in this dialogue for
control programs creation, to continue the work push ОК button. If no system is se-
lected, a warning will appear on the screen (fig. 1.2).
1.2.  
If module is not run, further work will be impossible. After control program selec-
tion, 
BAZIS-CNC  window (see section 1.2 on p. 17) will appear on the screen.
Common parameters are set in Settings dialogue. It will appear on the screen after
Settings – Parameters command call. Common parameters setup can be execut-
ed from any module. It is described in detail in BAZIS system Installation and setup.
Control programs setting is executed in Preview for CNC window (see Chapter 7
on p. 40). Control programs generation can be started from BAZIS-CNC as well as
from other BAZIS system modules. For this purpose push 
BAZIS-CNC  button in
BAZIS module window.
Fig. 1.1.  
Fig. 1.2.

[Página 17]
17
Chapter 1.
1.2. BAZIS-CNC window
After module start and postprocessor selection BAZIS-CNC window will appear on
the screen. Window control elements allow to operate with furniture model, control
its viewing, set and create control programs for the model. The module is Windows
application. Its window and its control el ements are standard for operating system
(fig. 1.3).
1.3.  
Interface elements concise description is shown in tab. 1.1.
Fig. 1.3.

Main menu Contains module commands grouped in sections (menu).
Toolbars Contain command call buttons.
Auxiliary bar 
Librarian
Contains control elements for BAZIS system libraries work. 
Model window Contains model image for which control programs are 
created.

1.3. Librarian auxiliary panel
Librarian auxiliary panel control elements (fig. 1.4) allow to work with BAZIS sys-
tem libraries.
1.4.  
Library can be created in the following way:
▼ automatically, when you create assembly drawing or documents from a model,
▼ via Librarian auxiliary panel control elements.
BAZIS system libraries use is described in detail in Work with libraries and projects
User Guide.
1.4. Interface elements state control
Interface elements, except Title bar and documents windows, may be in two states:
▼ pinned,
▼ floating.
In pinned state interface element is attached to module window bound. At window
moving or its size changing, interface elements are moved together with attachment
bound. In floating state, interface element can be moved to any place on the screen,
including outside window bound. Thus, extra place for geometric constructions is
free.
Cursor current 
coordinates 
X, Y and Z fields contain current cursor coordinates in a 
module window. Z field is available if current model is a 3D 
object.
Tab. 1.1. BAZIS-CNC interface elements
Name Description
Fig. 1.4.

[Página 19]
19
Chapter 1.
1.5. Toolbars arrangement
Toolbars, Main menu and Message bar arrangement is fulfilled in the same way. For
setting description in this section, the only term is used – a toolbar. In pinned state
toolbars are placed in the following wa y. Toolbars buttons and menu names are
placed in one line, bars are fixed at upper or lower window bound. The title of pinned
bar is not viewed. Instead of it, a vertical line appears on the left bar bound. At cursor
dragging to this line, it becomes a quad rilateral arrow. To change bar placement,
click left mouse button on the vertical line, and holding it, drag the bar to necessary
place.
It you drag the bar to some place from the window bound, it becomes floating; it gets
a title. Double click on vertical line changes the toolbar state to floating. It is untied
from the window bound.
Toolbar size in floating state can be changed. The buttons and names will be auto-
matically placed in several lines according to bar size.
For toolbar position in floating state change, it is necessary to click on its title with
left mouse button, and holding it, drag the bar to necessary place. At bar dragging
to upper or lower window bound, it is at tracted and attached to it. Bar title disap-
pears. At double click on a title of a floati ng toolbar, it is automatically attached to
upper window bound. After 
Fix command call, toolbar dragging to window bound
does not pin it. Double click on bar tittle allows to pin it.
Settings – Toolbars section commands help control bars visibility. Enabled option
next to bar name shows, that bar is visualized in a window. For toolbar hiding, click
its name in a menu. The bar will disappear, menu option in next to bar name will be
disabled. If the toolbar is in floating state, to hide the bar push standard 
Close button
in bar title.
1.6. Desktop control
The set of displayed interface elements , their sizes and position is called desktop.
Current desktop settings are saved automatically in UserInterface.xml file. Desktop
configuration may be saved for further use.  E.g. you can create desktops for work
with drawings and models, import the configuration to other desktops, etc. For con-
figuration saving, call 
Settings – Save settings command.
A standard Windows dialogue will appear  on the screen. Configuration file name
should be input in this dialogue. The file  automatically gets file name extension
bzconfig. Desktop settings are saved with other current module settings. After com-
mand completion, a message will appear on the screen (fig. 1.5).
Settings – Toolbar – Fix command disables bar state changing. After command
call, option next to command name will be activated; it shows the command state.
Vertical line of pinned bars disappears, their dragging becomes impossible. 
Fix
command can be called from context menu.

1.5.  
There are settings file names in this message saved in configuration file:
▼ CNCUserInterface.xml – interface settings.
▼ CNCSettings.xml – basic module settings fulfilled in Settings dialogue (see BAZIS
system Installation and setup).
▼ Settings.xml – common settings of BAZIS system.
▼ NC500.pstconfig.xml – parameters settings of selected machine control program.

essary to select a file of required configuration in this dialogue.
1.7. Skins

mode. Skin names submenu will appear after command call. Option next to current
skin name is activated. For skin selectio n click on its name. Window view will be
changed; option next to selected name will be activated.

3.1. Document opening
BAZIS-CNC module is a multiwindow application. Several models can be opened in
it simultaneously for control programs generation. Models can be opened from
model files or BAZIS libraries and from th e projects. If module is started from BA-
ZIS-Woodworker or BAZIS-Cabinet modules, current model from these modules
will be opened in it automatically.

To open furniture model for control programs generation, call File – Open com-
mand. A standard Windows dialogue will appear on the screen. Control programs
data can be generated for models saved in files. These types are listed in tab. 3.1.
In this dialogue select a model file or model libraries of BAZIS system.
Viewing button allows to control model icon viewing. Opened files list is saved in
the module. List size is set at setup. Button with downward arrow allows to open a
list for file selection. After file opening a document window will appear on the
screen.
3.1.2. Model from document library opening
Models for control programs generation can be opened from BAZIS system librar-
ies. Libraries use is described in detail in Work with libraries and projects User
Guide. To open library use Librarian auxiliary panel control elements or File –

After library file opening its content will be shown on Librarian auxiliary panel

Cursor selection
Single objects cursor selection can be customized in the module settings (see BA-
ZIS system Installation and setup ). Cursor or Shift+LeftMouse Click selection
modes should be chosen as most convenient. Select an object by left mouse click.
To select several objects with a cursor hold <Ctrl> button pushed.
Frame selection
If you need to select a group of objects with a frame, push <Shift> button and hold-
ing left mouse click in blank space of the drawing. Thus you specify frame first ver-
tex position. Holding the buttons drag the cursor in the drawing window. Frame se-
lection phantom will appear on the screen. Then specify the second vertex position
in a way, that the rectangle will embrace all selected objects.
Objects of specified material selection
If you need to select the objects made of some specified material, call Selection –
Select by material command. Objects selection dialogue will appear on the
screen (fig. 4.2).

name allows to choose material for selection. To select the objects with chosen ma-
terials push 
ОК button. Cancel button closes the dialogue without changes.

Chapter 5.Drawings use for control programs generation
5.1. Drawing execution demands
5.1.1. General information
Control programs can be created for models in BAZIS system sheets format and
saved in ldw file. Panel drawing should be executed in particular way – panel objects
should be arranged on separate layers. Separate layers should be created for the fol-
lowing objects:
▼ panel contour,
▼ face holes,
▼ edge holes,
▼ slots on a face,
▼ edge slots.
If there are several objects of the same type  in the panel, e.g. face holes, but these
holes have different radius values, a separate layer should be created for each radi-
us. Layer names should consist of the following parts.
▼ Prefix – arbitrary symbols. It is reasonable to set prefix that corresponds to layer ob-
ject type, e.g. CONTOUR layer will contain panel contour image, HOLE_FACE layer –
face holes images, etc.
▼ Layer object parameter value, e.g. panel thickness, hole depth, slot width and depth.
5.1.2. Layers properties
Contour
Contour parameter is panel thickness in millimeters. Panel contour may contain line,
arc and circle segments. Contour should be closed. Contour lines are visualized with
continuous line. Name example of panel layer of 16 mm thickness: CONTOUR16.
Face holes
Hole parameter is its depth value. If hole is through, it is reasonable to use value that
exceeds panel thickness by 2...4 mm. Face  holes layer number should correspond
to different holes depth values. Holes are visualized with circles of different diame-
ter. Face holes are visualized with continuous line, back side holes – with dashed
line (fig. 5.1).
Layer name prefix for the same objects shou ld be the same, i.e. for face holes of 4
and 10 mm diameter it is necessary to create layers with names HOLE_FACE4 and
HOLE_FACE10.

[Página 34]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
34
5.1.  
Name example of face blind holes layer of 8 mm depth: HOLE_FACE8. Name exam-
ple of panel through holes layer of 16 mm thickness: HOLE_FACE20.
Edge holes
Edge hole parameter is distance between front face and Z hole axis (fig. 5.2).
5.2.  
Edge holes layer number should correspond to Z parameter values number. Hole is
visualized with a rectangle, one of its si de coincide with contour line. Sides length
are equal to hole diameter and depth. Rectangle is visualized with dashed line
(fig. 5.3).
5.3.  
Name example of edge holes layer which distance between axis and front face is
equal to 8 mm: HOLE_EDGE8.

Chapter 5.
Slots on a face
Slot parameters are width and depth values. Parameters in layer name are separated
with symbol / (<width>/<depth>). Face slot layer number should correspond to slots
of different sizes number. Slots are visualized with closed or open-ended contour. It
may contain line, arc and circle segments. Contour lines on front side are visualized
with continuous line, on back side – with dashed line (fig. 5.4).
5.4.  
Name example of slot on a face layer of 10 mm width and 5 mm depth:
SLOT_FACE10/5.
Edge slots
Slot parameters are width value and distance between front face and the nearest Z
slot bound (fig. 5.5).
5.5.  
Parameters in layer name are separated with symbol / ( <width>/<Z value>). Face
slot layer number should correspond to slots of different size number. Slots are vi-
sualized with closed or open-ended contour. It may contain line, arc and circle seg-
ments. Slot depth is constant, thus slot  contour should be equidistant from panel
contour. The distance between contour lines corresponds to slot depth. Slot contour
is visualized with continuous line (fig. 5.6).

Chapter 6.Data preparation for control programs creation
6.1. General information
To create control programs for current model push BAZIS-CNC  button on Mod-
ules toolbar in BAZIS-CNC  window. To create control programs for the current
model, set input data format. Settings are specified by document type.
6.2. Drawing data preparation
After BAZIS-CNC  button pushing, Input data dialogue will appear on the screen
(fig. 6.1).
6.1.  
To prepare data push Layers format button. Layers format under drawing
reading dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 6.2).
6.2.  
6.2.1. Data types to layer name prefix mapping
Information contained in a layer group contains data types that are used in a
module for control programs creation. Each data type should be mapped to layer
name prefix that contains corresponding data.
Prefix group contains layer names combo boxes. These names are imported from
a drawing. To map layer prefix to data type do the following.
Fig. 6.1.  
Fig. 6.2.  
Only layer name prefix will be mapped. Data from all layers with the same type ob-
jects will be grouped, e.g. face holes with different diameter.

[Página 38]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
38
1. Select layer name from a combo box that contains the objects that correspond to
current data type.
Several layers with the same name prefix and different parameters may correspond
to one data type. You can select any name. E.g. blind holes of 8 mm depth and
through holes should be bored in panel face. Layers with names are created for their
visualization, HOLE_FACE8 and HOLE_FACE20. Let HOLE_FACE8 layer be selected
for instance.
2. Click with mouse left button in a layer name line.
Text cursor will appear in a string.
3. Delete parameter values so that prefix will remain, in the example, HOLE_FACE.
Then map all data types to drawing layers prefixes in the same way.
6.2.2. Separation symbol of decimal part
Separation symbol field allows to set a sign that separates integer part from frac-
tional part in layer parameter designation. Сomma is used as a separator in Russian
designations, in English – a point. Thus, to set a layer parameter that contains holes
of 6,5 mm diameter, add 6,5 string to prefix. In some CAD, e.g. AutoCAD, no com-
mas and points are allowed in layer names. Thus layers imported from such systems
will contain a string, e.g. 6_5. To make the module interpret parameter in layer name
correctly, specify a symbol as decimal separator. Thus, if drawing is imported from
AutoCAD and an underscore <_> character is used in layer names as a separator,
this very sign should be input in 
Separation symbol field.
6.2.3. Setup completion
To complete layer prefixes setup push ОК button. Cancel button allows to close the
dialogue without changes. Layers format under drawing reading dialogue will
be closed.
6.3. Data from a model preparation
After pushing BAZIS-CNC button and if separate panels were selected in a model,
Input data dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 6.3).
6.3.  
If none of the objects was selected, this message will not appear. Save panels
from a model group variants allow to select the panels for processing.
Fig. 6.3.

Chapter 7.Preview for CNC window
7.1. General information
Control programs are generated in Preview for CNC  window (fig. 7.1 on p. 41).
Current model file name is shown in window title bar.
This window will appear on the screen after layers or drawing prefixes setup or mod-
el panels selection completion, and pushing ОК button in Input data dialogue.
7.2. Control programs general information
7.2.1. Order name
Order field allows to input order name. Name may contain arbitrary text. When you
open a project, project file name will appear in a field automatically.
7.2.2. Product count
Product count field allows to set panels count. By default one panels set will be
processed. If you change field value, Count fields values will be changed automat-
ically.
7.2.3. Configuration choice
Configuration combo box contains configuration names. Each configuration is a
set of the following parameters:
▼ model reading parameters,
▼ technological parameters, that are specified by machine control system type,
▼ panels list that require control programs creation.
Several configurations can be created for each control system. They may differ in
e.g. operations (boring, milling) or processed panels list. Configurations are created
for each control system chosen in Choose machine control system dialogue (see
section 1.1 on p. 16). When you choose one of them, this very configuration data
will appear on window tabs.
7.3. Panel list
When you open Preview for CNC window, panel lists for all configurations will be
generated automatically. Current configuration list is viewed on Panel list  tab
(fig. 7.1).
If model or drawing are transferred from ot her modules, temporary file will be cre-
ated, its name is viewed in a title bar.

tion coincides with entered value. One of  the panels is marked. It can be moved
along the list via a mouse or cursor cont rol buttons. Selected panel contour is

8.3. Holes editing
Holes editing button allows to change holes parameters on selected panel. After
pushing the button, Holes editing dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 8.2).
8.2.  
There is edited panel and holes list in this  dialogue. When you select a list string,
hole image will be highlighted. The following values will be shown for each hole: di-
ameter, depth, center coordinates, layout side, belonging to accessories.
8.3.1. Hole adding
Add button allows to add a hole. After pushing the button diameter, depth and new
hole center coordinates fields will become available. Fill the fields, to finish hole add-
ing, push ОК button to the right of entry fields. Hole string will appear in a list, its
image will appear on a panel. Cancel button allows to cancel editing.
8.3.2. Holes editing
Edit button allows to edit hole diameter and selected hole depth. To finish editing
push ОК button to the right of entry fields. Cancel button allows to cancel editing.
8.3.3. Holes deletion
Delete button allows to delete selected ho le. After pushing the button, a warning
will appear on the screen (fig. 8.3).
8.3.  
Message buttons allow to confirm deletion or reject it.
8.3.4. Holes editing completion
To complete holes editing push ОК button of Holes editing dialogue. Cancel but-
ton allows to close the dialogue without changes.

8.4. Contour milling control
Point contour segments for milling button allows to specify panel contour seg-
ments for milling and set milling order. After pushing the button Point contour
segments for milling dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 8.4).
8.4.  
To specify a contour segment for milling, click its image with mouse left button.
Milling segment in Panel contour list is designated with an option. Select all and
Undo all buttons allow to control all contour segments selection and unselection.
Processing beginning button allows to set contour segment for milling start. Se-
lected segment will be automatically moved  to list beginning and marked with an
icon.
Scroll wheel allows to scale the panel image. Show all button allows to view a panel
in a way, that it will fit a preview panel in maximum scale.
To complete contour segments milling setup push ОК button. Point contour seg-
ments for milling dialogue will be closed. A dagger will be upper in Machining
contour custom setting cell. Cancel button allows to close the dialogue without
changes.
8.5. Slots editing
Trajectory direction and work sequence of slots button allows to operate with
selected panel slots. After pushing the button Trajectory direction and work se-
quence of slots dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 8.5).

Chapter 8.
8.5.  
There is a current panel on dialogue viewing panel. Slot names are in the list. One
of the names can be selected with mouse left button. Slot image will be also select-
ed.
Up and Down buttons allow to move selected name along the list. Slots processing
will be changed correspondingly.
Scroll wheel allows to scale the panel image. Show all button allows to view the
panel in a way, that it will fit the viewing panel in maximum scale.
Processing direction is viewed by an arrow on a slot line. Change direction button
allows to change processing direction of selected slot to the opposite.

8.6. Mirrored view
Transform symmetrically about horizontal axis and Transform symmetri-
cally about vertical axis buttons allow to get current panel mirrored view about
horizontal and vertical axes respectively.
In panel Mirrored view cell a dagger will appear.
8.7. Panel rotation
Panel image in viewing area and a panel on machine work area can be rotated. Enter
angle value in Rotate board on field. Combo box variants allow to rotate in 0°, 90°,
180° and 270°. If rotation angle is set by the user, the following additional values
will appear in a combo box:
▼ user angle negative value allows to restore panel initial position,
▼ value that completes the angle to 90°.
Angles combo box for user angle of 60° is in fig. 8.7.
8.7.  
In some cases, rotated panel processing is impossible. To define such situations,
set operations checking (see section 9.4 on p. 60).
8.8. Panels in list visualization
8.8.1. General information
Selected panel is viewed on viewing panel as a map. Panel chart view parame-
ters button allows to set this image. After pushing the button Panel chart view
parameters dialogue will appear on the screen.
8.8.2. Edge banding visualization
Banding section control elements (fig. 8.8) allow to set edge banding visualization
parameters.
Fig. 8.7.

[Página 49]
49
Chapter 8.
8.8.  
Banding is visualized with a colour line. A line colour can be set by banding material
thickness or its name.
Colour selection by thickness
If By thickness variant is selected, banding line colour will be specified by banding
material thickness. For each thickness range you should select the colour from a
combo box. Browse button allows to select additional colours. After pushing the
button, you can select the colour in a standard Windows dialogue.
Colour selection by material name
If By name variant is selected, banding line colour will be specified by banding ma-
terial name. The section table contains banding material names, that are used in the
current model. On\Off option allows to control particular materials visualization.
To specify the line colour for the current material, click the rectangle with mouse left
button in Display colour field and select it from a combo box of standard colours.
If it is necessary to set specification colour, push Browse button and select the co-
lour in a standard Windows dialogue.
8.8.3. Holes and slots line colour setup
Holes and slots line colour setup control elements are placed in corresponding
Panel chart view parameters dialogue sections. These settings function in the
same way and they are described in holes setting example (fig. 8.9).
8.9.  
Line colour of each hole type is viewed in a rectangle. To change it, select it from
standard colours combo box. If you need to set specific colour, push 
Browse button
and select colour in a standard Windows dialogue.

will appear on the screen (fig. 8.10).

file. To apply these settings reload the pa nels list. A hint will appear on the screen
(fig. 8.11).

9.1.3. File name template
Control program file name is created automatically according to set template. Tem-
plate content is shown in Name template field. To set a template push Browse
button. File name template dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 9.3).
9.3.  
Template elements
Element names that can be included into a file name are shown in Parameters list.
When you save files, these parameters values from a model will be used instead of
them. To add an element to the template, select it in the list and push Add param-
eter to template button. An element will appear in Template parameters col-
umn. Set character count in Character count field.
Delete parameter from template button allows to delete selected element from
a template.
Originally the elements are placed in Template parameters list in addition order.
Move up and Move down buttons allow to move selected element along the list.
For Separator element Value field is available. You can input a symbol that sepa-
rates other elements. Serial Number parameter is used for control programs file
names creation taking into account the number of parts of one item.
Template setup
Cyrillic symbols can be automatically changed to Latin. For this purpose check in
Transliteration to Latin field. Space in file name is unwanted, some symbols can
not be used because of Windows file system restrictions. Replace spaces with and
Fig. 9.3.

[Página 53]
53
Chapter 9.
Replace reserved characters with fields allow to set the symbols for their re-
placement. If symbols count is set for parameter saving and parameter value length
is less than this count, additional symbol s will be added to a file name. By default
zeros will be added. Add symbol field allows to set another additional symbol.
If it is necessary to process both panel f aces, two files of control programs will be
created. Side designation may be one of file name elements. Create for face com-
bo box variants in Side designation group allow to choose designation creation
condition. Set corresponding designations in Face and Back fields. There is created
template viewing string in the lower dial ogue part. To can choose colour for tem-
plate elements with different parameters vi sualization. Individual colour can be set
for variable length parameters, fixed length (with set Character count parameter)
and for separator. Select colour in corresponding combo boxes. If necessary you
can select additional colours. For this purpose push 
Browse button and select co-
lour in a standard Windows dialogue. To complete file name template setup, push
ОК button. Cancel button allows to close the dialogue without changes.
9.2. Parameters saving and loading
A set of current configuration parameters can be saved in a file. Push Save config-
uration parameters button and set a file name in a standard Windows dialogue. A
file will have cncconfig extension.
To load a set of parameters from a file, push Load configuration parameters but-
ton and choose a file with cncconfig extension in a standard Windows dialogue.
9.3. Data reading setup
Configuration parameters button allows to set current configuration parameters.
Data from a model reading parameters are the same for all control systems. Tech-
nological parameters setup is specific for each control system and it is described in
Chapter 10 on p. 63. Model reading parameters are set on 
Reading parameters
tab of Configuration parameters dialogue.
9.3.1. Common parameters
Common section control elements allow to set panels reading parameters
(fig. 9.4).

[Página 54]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
54
9.4.  
Panel orientation
Control program is created for particular panel position on machine fences. It is nec-
essary to take into account machine desktop dimensions and configuration and
panel overall size. To set panel position use 
Panel orientation group variants.
If From model variant is selected, panel position on a desktop will correspond to
its position in a model.
Longer side along X axis variant allows to place the panel in a way, that its long
side will be horizontally placed.
Longer side along Y axis variant allows to place the panel in a way, that long side
will be vertically placed.
On condition Y<= 1.5 X variant allows to place the panel in a way, so that the side
that is one and a half times larger than the other side is positioned vertically.
By texture direction along X axis and along Y variants allow to take material tex-
ture direction into account. The panel will be placed in a way, that its texture will be
parallel to selected axis.
Front side selection
The panel is arranged on machine desktop in a way, that processed surfaces will be
available for a tool. If slots or blind holes are placed on both panel faces, two control
Fig. 9.4.  
Setting data work for all panels of the model. Panels orientation can be changed (see
section 8.7 on p. 48).

[Página 55]
55
Chapter 9.
programs will be created for them. Control programs design job will contain two im-
ages of such panel. One of the panel faces is called front.
Panel face side choice criteria group variants allow to assign the panels that will
be considered front panels automatically.
If Face side specified variant is selected, then front side is assigned the area that
is assigned in the panel properties. If By default from model variant is selected,
front side will be the face that is arranged in the following ways in a model:
▼ horizontal – above,
▼ vertical – from the right,
▼ front – from the front.
More blind holes, Less blind hole s, More through holes, More slots variants
allow to assign a front panel according to selected criterion. Up and Down buttons
allow to move criteria through the list. The panel sides are checked for compliance
with the criteria in turn, according to th e list. The check is terminated if the side
meets the next criterion.
Back face position
If there are two panel images in panels lis t, panel back side image will be marked
with a sign in Back face cell. The panel should be rotated to the user from the pro-
cessed side. Initially the panel is arranged in a way as it is placed in a model. If pro-
cessed side is turned up, no extra actions are required. If it is turned down, the panel
should be turned over, i.e. visualize its opposite side. X axis and Y axis variants of
Back face is created relatively to group allow to select an axis for panel rotation.
If processed from the single side, consider back side as face one option allows
to exclude back face symbol from side designations, even if this side is such.
Panel joining
Parts that are mirror image of each other can be joined for processing. One of the
panels is placed on another. Panel join criteria group control elements allow to se-
lect parameters, if they coincide th e panels will be joined automatically. Same
(multiply 2) option allows to join the same panels, if their number is a multiple of
two.
Project panels designation
If control program is created for project with several products, it is reasonable to add
project and product name to it at automatic panel designation creation. For this pur-
pose use Add project(s) designation to panel position option. If you disregard
it, panels list will contain panels with the same designations.
The side of through hole boring is set at accessories mounting and editing com-
mands. Accessories work is described in BAZIS-Woodworker module User Guide.

[Página 56]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
56
Project designation elements separator field allows to select corresponding
symbol. Too long position designation can be rather inconvenient. In this case you
can reject supplementary information automatic adding to its designation. Product
and project designation is not added if position symbol count exceeds field
allows to set position designation maximum size.

9.3.3. Hole parameters
Holes section control elements allow to set panel holes processing (fig. 9.6).
Fig. 9.5.

[Página 57]
57
Chapter 9.
9.6.  
If Opposite blind holes from different panel faces merge to a through one
option is enabled, coaxial blind holes that intersect by depth will be merged to
through hole. It slows to bore only from one face. Boring may lead to splinters on
the face back side, especially if a drill is dull. Through hole is divided into pair of
blind ones from different faces option allows to bore different faces by-turn
without damage. Through hole division into two blind holes is not obligatory for all
holes. To specify the diameter of holes for boring from both sides push Through
holes diameter button. It is available if the option is enabled. After pushing the but-
ton, Through holes diameter dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 9.7).
9.7.  
Diameter field allows to set diameter value of  divided hole. To add a blank line to
the table push Add button. Delete button allows to delete the current diameter table
line. To complete diameter list of divided holes creation push ОК button. Cancel
Fig. 9.6.  
Fig. 9.7.

[Página 58]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
58
button allows to close the dialogue without changes. Drilling depth overlapping
field allows to set holes bore depth overlapping that ensures through hole creation.
Machines may bore from one side as well as from both panel sides. These peculiar-
ities specify control programs creation order. If boring is executed from one side, it
is necessary to create control program for boring from both sides. Two separate
files will be created. To set such control programs creation order select 
Create two
files variant in Installations (files) for one panel count group. If you enable If
edge holes exist option, additional condition of two files creation will be set.
If two files creation variant is selected, Drill through holes from back side (in the
second file) option will become available. It al lows to add operations of through
holes boring to the second program part, i.e. from back panel face. Duplicate edge
holes in the second file option allows to duplicate edge holes.
Machine may stop the operation so that the operator can turn over the panel for sec-
ond side processing. In this case one program for both faces processing will be cre-
ated. To set such order of control programs creation, select Create single file vari-
ant. This variant should be selected if machine may process both faces at once.
There can be holes of different diameters in a model. Some of them, e.g. nail holes,
are designated on a drawing, but they are not bored. Process hole with diameter
more than field allows to set minimum holes diameter value. Process holes with
diameter less than field allows to set holes diameter maximum value. Drill blind
holes with drills for through holes if depth is less than field allows to set blind
holes depth for drills for through holes use.
9.3.4. Banding accounting
Banding accounting section control elements (fig. 9.8) allow to control panel
edge banding programs accounting.

[Página 59]
59
Chapter 9.
9.8.  
Remove allowance option allows to add allowance remove on panel edges oper-
ations to control programs. If it is enabled, allowance will be removed in further
technological operations. Panel can be processed on CNC machine with banded
edges. Thus, holes, slots and panel internal contours may be referenced from panel
bound or from banded edge bound. To select a necessary variant push a button with
a corresponding icon in 
By default group. If referencing is executed from banded
bound, you can specify the panel types for this setting application. For this purpose
use For all panels or For rectangle panels variants.
9.3.5. Slots analysis
Slots section control elements (fig. 9.9) allow to set slots trajectory construction on
a panel.
Fig. 9.8.

[Página 60]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide

9.9.  
Linear horizontal slot direction and Linear vertical slot direction groups vari-
ants allow to select corresponding slots processing direction. If the panel is rotated
or mirrored, slot processing direction will be changed. Do not change slot direc-
tion if panel is rotated option allows to control selected direction fixing. The panel
can be rotated in consideration of slot position. Place panel with horizontal slot
group variants allow take this parameter into account. If by default variant is se-
lected, slot position will not be taken into account, the panel will be placed according
to other reading settings.
Slots can be excluded from processing or included into it by slot name mask. To cre-
ate a mask of names selection input it in a field. You can use substitutes in mask.
Asterix <*> replaces any number of symbols. There can be several masks. To add a
mask to the list, push 
Add button.
Delete button allows to exclude a mask from the list.
To edit selected mask push Edit button.
9.4. Check operations realizability
9.4.1. General information
Operations realizability check setup relates to other parameters and is performed in
Satisfiability check section on Miscellaneous parameters tab (fig. 9.10). This
setup is the same for all control systems.
Fig. 9.9.

[Página 61]
61
Chapter 9.
The check allows to specify the ability to create control programs depending on con-
figuration of machine work area and panel size. If control programs creation for cur-
rent parameters is impossible, a warning will appear in module window, and panels
designations that cause the errors will be highlighted. To eliminate the errors,
change the corresponding parameter. For example, if the current position of the
panel does not fit on the desktop, rotate it in the corresponding way (see section 8.7
on p. 48).
9.10.  
9.4.2. Checked operations
Check satisfiability on work zone area option allows to control check perform-
ing. Options in operations names, e.g. Drilling into a face, allow to include opera-
tions in the number of checked ones.
9.4.3. Checked dimensions
Panel size
Length and Width fields of Minimum workpiece dimensions group allow to set
minimum panel dimensions that can be processed on the machine. To check the
panel, you should enable the corresponding option.
Fig. 9.10.  
Checked operations content depends on th e design and technical capabilities of a
particular machine. That is, if the machine is not designed for milling, this operation
will be omitted from the list.

[Página 62]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
62
9.4.4. Reference point position
Reference point position of machine work area is determined by its design. It can be
located at one of the vertexes of overall rectangle. During control programs creating
dimensions are based from this very point. The position of origin of coordinates is
taken into account when checking the satisfiability of operations. To set its position,
select one of 
Origin of coordinates placement angle group variants.
9.5. Panel image saving parameters
Panel images can be saved to files. Sa ving parameters setup is performed in Pre-
view section on Miscellaneous parameters tab (fig. 9.11). This setting is the
same for all control systems.
9.11.  
To save panel images, enable Save panel contour image option. Image rotation
combo box allows to select image rotation angle, 0° or 90°. File format combo box
allows to select image saving format, JPEG or WMF. Show edge banding option
allows to control banding visualization in saved images. Save control program
name option allows to add control program file name to panel image.

Chapter 10.Process-dependent parameters setup
10.1. General information
Set parameters for each machine for au tomatic control programs creation. Pro-
grams for some machine types can be created in two formats, e.g. in machine con-
trol program format and in AutoCad format. This format can be read by control sys-
tem but some parameters can be absent, e.g. correction type, etc. To set program
parameters use control elements on 
Process-dependent parameters  tab of
Configuration parameters dialogue.
10.2. Saved parameters loading
Load parameters button is available for all control systems. It allows to load con-
figuration parameters generated in BAZIS-CNC of previous version. After pushing
the button select a file with npr extension in a standard Windows dialogue.

Chapter 10.
Contour processing parameters
Outer and internal contours processing parameters are set on Milling tab
(fig. 10.7).
10.7.  
Tool combo box allows to select a tool milling from the list prepared on Tools tab.
Tool lists should be created in Tools section. Execution of the whole contour op-
tion allows to set the elements that will be milled. If it is enabled, all panel edges will
be milled. If it is disabled, the edges that do not coincide with overall rectangle sides
will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular and the option is disabled, contour will not
be milled. Path direction group variants of outer and internal contour allow to se-
lect tool moving direction.
Hole parameters
Holes processing is set in Hole section (fig. 10.8).
Fig. 10.7.

[Página 68]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
68
10.8.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.

alogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.10).

milling. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled. If it is disabled, the edges that
do not coincide with panel overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rect-
angular, and the option is disabled, contour will not be milled.

allows to activate this operation. Group fields allow to set option parameters. Mill-
ing path direction group variants allow to select tool moving direction.

Contour parameters
Contour milling parameters are set in Outer contour (fig. 10.35) and Internal
contour sections of Process-dependent parameters tab.
10.35.  
Tabs control elements allow to select a tool, correction types, tool lead-in and lead-
out, etc. Tab control elements sets are almost the same. There are additional control
elements on 
Outer contour tab. Execution of the whole contour option allows
to set the elements that will be milled. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled.
If it is disabled, the edges that do not coincide with overall rectangle sides will be
milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular and the option is disabled, contour will not be
milled.
Holes
Hole section control elements (fig. 10.36) allow to setup milling parameters.
Fig. 10.35.

[Página 87]
87
Chapter 10.
10.36.  
Blind hole drilling and Through hole drilling groups variants allow to select drill-
ing speed. If there are several holes on on e line and distance between them is the
same (step), it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this pur-
pose enable Group holes option. Holes can be bored with a drill as well as with a
mill. Hole diameter usually specifies tool selection. If diameter is more or equal
field allows to set hole diameter, if it exceeds, the hole will be milled automatically,
mill name is shown in Tool field. You can map hole and mill diameter that will be
automatically selected for its processing. For this purpose push Diameters button.
Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.37).
10.37.  
Dialogue table contains holes diameter values and corresponding mill designations.
The fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add row button allows to add a blank row
to the table.
To delete the current row push Delete row button. Arrow buttons allow to change
current row position in the table. To complete setup push ОК button. Cancel button
allows to close the dialogue without changes.
Fig. 10.36.  
Fig. 10.37.

tool of only one type. If you try to add a tool of another type, a warning will appear
on the screen (fig. 10.40).

do not coincide with panel overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rect-

Hole parameters
Holes processing is set in Hole section (fig. 10.50).
10.50.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. For edge holes use Z and For holes on a face apply Z
combo boxes variants allow to choose a face for this parameter application. Tool
lifting between operations field allows to set tool height. Section table allows to
set automatic spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill
shortest path and on diameter variants of Drill holes on a face group allow to
set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.
Fig. 10.50.

[Página 96]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
96

10.51.

ear grooving tool combo boxes allow to choose a tool for corresponding opera-
tions. Tool list should be created beforehand. Tool choice button allows to set tools
for particular slots processing. After pushing the button Tool choice dialogue will
appear on the screen (fig. 10.52).
10.52.  
Slot name list contains slot names of the model. Add mill button allows to map
selected slot with a tool from the list. Several tools can be assigned for one slot.
Delete button allows to delete selected tool from the list. To complete tool assign-
ment push ОК button. Cancel button allows to close the dialogue without changes.
Lead in and lead out length , Lead-in angle section fields allow to set supple-
mentary parameters of slot processing.
Fig. 10.51.  
Fig. 10.52.

point group control elements allow to cont rol tool return point position. If To co-
ordinate system origin option is enabled, tool will be returned to this point. If it is
disabled, return point coordinates are set by X, dY and dZ fields values. Tool lifting
between operations field allows to set tool height. Base reference area combo
box variants allow to set base reference area position. Y base point field allows to
set base point position.

Tool combo box allows to select a milling tool name. Tools should be created be-

count, etc. Execution of the whole contour option allows to set the elements for
milling. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled. If it is disabled, the edges that
do not coincide with panel overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rect-
angular, and it is disabled, contour will not be milled. Path direction group variants
allow to select tool shift direction.

10.80.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance.
For edge holes use Z and For holes on a face apply Z combo boxes variants al-
low to choose a face for this parameter application. Tool lifting between opera-
tions field allows to set tool height. Section table allows to set automatic spindle
number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path and on
diameter variants of Drill holes on a face group allow to set holes boring order.

[Página 139]

10.113.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. For edge holes use Z and For holes on a face apply Z
combo boxes variants allow to choose a face for this parameter application. Tool
lifting between operations field allows to set tool height.
Section table allows to set automatic spindle number selection depending on hole
parameters. On drill shortest path and on diameter variants of Drill holes on a
face group allow to set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the center of the hole, then there will be no such defects. 
Through hole milling
from the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the
center or on the diameter of the hole.

Contours parameters
Contours milling parameters are set in Outer contour (fig. 10.119) and Internal
contour sections of Process-dependent parameters tab.
10.119.  
Finish machining and Rough machining tabs control elements allow to select a
tool, correction types, tool lead-in and lead-out, etc. Rough machining option al-
lows to control this operation. Control elements of these tabs are almost the same.
There are additional control elements on Outer contour tab. Execution of the
whole contour option allows to set the elements that will be milled. If it is enabled,
all panel edges will be milled. If it is di sabled, the edges that do not coincide with
overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular and the option is dis-
abled, contour will not be milled.

10.120.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option. Through holes can be bored with drills for through and blind
holes. Drill type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have
center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed
material thickness. Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group
allows to take drill center point into account. Tool numbers table allows to set au-
tomatic drill selection depending on hole type and diameter. This setting will work
if Set tool numbers option is enabled. You can map hole and mill diameter that will
be automatically selected for its processing. For this purpose push Diameters but-
ton. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.121).
10.121.  
Dialogue table contains holes diameter values and corresponding mill designations.
The fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add row button allows to add a blank row
to the table.

Fig. 10.121.

lows to close the dialogue without changes. If a hole is processed with a mill, you
can select correction type from 
Correction combo box for particular mill diameter
set in Diameters field. For edge holes use Z group variants allow to select a face
for this parameter application.

ues. Tool lifting between operations field allows to set tool height. Work area
combo box variants allow to set work area coordinate axes direction.

angular, and the option is disabled, contour will not be milled. Zigzag lead in option

control holes with a mill processing order, from center or from perimeter.

parameters. On drill shortest path and on diameter variants of Drill holes on a

10.154.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option. Through holes can be bored with drills for through and blind
holes. Drill type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have
center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed
material thickness. 
Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group
allows to take drill center point into account.
Holes can be bored with a drill as well as with a mill. Hole diameter usually specifies
tool selection. Hole diameter is greater field allows to set hole diameter that spec-
ifies automatic tool selection in program. Set drill type NORMALEG and Process
by mill variants allow to select a tool type when set diameter value is excessed. If
Process by mill variant is selected, input a tool name and correction types, mill
lead-in and lead-out in corresponding fields. You can map hole and mill diameter
that will be automatically selected for its processing. For this purpose push 
Diam-
eter button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.155).

10.205.

Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes processing (fig. 10.206).
10.206.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.

10.216.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. If there are
several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step), it is possi-
ble to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable 
Group
holes option.

and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled. Holes
can be bored with a drill as well as with a mill. You can map hole and mill diameter
that will be automatically selected for its processing. For this purpose push Diam-
eter button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.217).

to the table.

Allowance for part section control elements allow to set allowance values. Sec-
tion fields allow to set allowance values for all panel sides. If The same from every
side option is enabled, the same from every panel edge allowance field will become
available. Append operations to CNC program group options allow to control

gram, enable an option next to its name.
Contours parameters
Contours milling parameters are set in Outer contour (fig. 10.252) and Internal
contour sections of Process-dependent parameters tab.
10.252.  
Finish machining and Rough machining tabs control elements allow to select a
tool, correction types, tool lead-in and lead-out, etc. Rough machining option al-
lows to control this operation. Control elements of these tabs are almost the same.
There are additional control elements on Outer contour tab. Execution of the
whole contour option allows to set the elements that will be milled. If it is enabled,
all panel edges will be milled. If it is di sabled, the edges that do not coincide with
overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular and the option is dis-
abled, contour will not be milled.
Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes parameters (fig. 10.253).
Fig. 10.252.

[Página 233]
233
Chapter 10.
10.253.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option. Through holes can be bore d with drills for through and blind
holes. Drill type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have
center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed
material thickness. Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group
allows to take drill center point into account.
Tool numbers table allows to set automatic drill selection depending on hole type
and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled. You can
map hole and mill diameter that will be automatically selected for its processing. For
this purpose push Diameters button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the
screen (fig. 10.254).
10.254.  
Dialogue table contains holes diameter values and corresponding mill designations.
The fields should be filled via a keyboard. 
Add row button allows to add a blank row
to the table.
Fig. 10.253.  
Fig. 10.254.

[Página 234]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
234
To delete the current row push Delete row button. Arrow buttons allow to change
current row position in the table. To finish setup push ОК button. Cancel button al-
lows to close the dialogue without changes. If a hole is processed with a mill, you
can select correction type from 
Correction combo box for particular mill diameter
set in Diameters field. For edge holes use Z group variants allow to select a face
for this parameter application.
Linear slot parameters
Linear slots parameters are set in Linear slot section (fig. 10.255).
10.255.  
Through slot always cut with saw  and Grooving by saw options allow to set
through slots direction that will be processed with a saw. Tool choice button allows
to set tools for particular slots processing. After pushing the button Tool choice di-
alogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.256).
10.256.  
Slot name table contains all slots names of the model. You can set particular mills
and a saw for each slot processing. Add mill button allows to add a tool to the list.
Mill name and its diameter can be edited in list fields. Correction type should be se-
lected from Correction combo box.
Fig. 10.255.  
Fig. 10.256.

Chapter 10.
Add saw button allows to choose a saw for slot processing.
The list may contain a tool of only one type. If you try to add a tool of another type,
a warning will appear on the screen (fig. 10.257).
10.257.  
Message buttons allow to delete a specified tool, clean the list or reject tool of an-
other type adding. Delete button allows to delete selected tool from the list. To
complete tool assignment push ОК button. Cancel button allows to close the dia-
logue without changes.
Grooving by saw parameters
Slot - by saw group control elements allow to set control programs creation pa-
rameters for grooving by saw – a tool, saw blade width and correction type.
Grooving by mill parameters
Slot - by mill group control elements allow to set control programs creation param-
eters for grooving by mill - a tool, mill di ameter, lead-in and lead-out parameters,
correction type, etc. You can map slot widt h and mill parameters that will be auto-
matically selected for its processing. For this purpose push Mill parameters but-
ton. Mill parameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.258).
10.258.  
Dialogue table contains slot width ra nges and corresponding mill parameters. The
fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add button allows to add a blank row to the
table. To delete the current row push Delete button. To finish parameters setup
push ОК button. The dialogue will be closed. Cancel button allows to close the di-
alogue without changes.

10.287.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.

Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes processing (fig. 10.298).
10.298.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
Fig. 10.298.

the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center

10.306.  
Tool combo box allows to select a milling tool name. Tools should be created be-
forehand in Tools section. Section control elements allow to set pass depth, pass
count, etc.
Execution of the whole contour option allows to set the elements for milling. If
it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled.  If it is disabled, the edges that do not
coincide with panel overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular,
and the option is disabled, contour will not be milled. Zigzag lead in option allows
to activate this operation. Group fields allow to set option parameters. Milling path
direction group variants allow to select tool moving direction.

10.307.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. If hole di-
ameter exceeds diameter of a mill, start from hole center option allows to
control holes with a mill processing order, from center or from perimeter.

allow to create a command list, that will be automatically moved to the specified pro-
gram areas. To process the panel, two separate files can be created containing pro-
grams for the front and non-front sides. Do not mill outer and internal contours
for non-front side processing file if there is a file for front side  option allows
to control milling program in non-front side file.

10.314.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. For edge holes use Z and For holes on a face apply Z
combo boxes variants allow to choose a face for this parameter application. Tool
lifting between operations field allows to set tool height. Section table allows to
set automatic spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill
shortest path and on diameter variants of Drill holes on a face group allow to
set holes boring order.

Hole parameters
Holes processing setup is performed in Hole section (fig. 10.332).
Fig. 10.331.

[Página 288]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
288
10.332.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance.
Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic spindle number selection
depending on hole parameters. 
On drill shortest path and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.

10.340.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option. Macros files names should be selected from a combo box.
Through holes can be bored with drills for through and blind holes. Drill type group
variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for
through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed material thickness. Ad-
ditional depth field in Through hole parameters group allows to take drill center
point into account.
Tool numbers table allows to set automatic drill selection depending on hole type
and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled. You can
map hole and mill diameter that will be automatically selected for its processing. For
this purpose push Diameters button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the
screen (fig. 10.341).

Chapter 10.
Reference point position group variants allow to select panel angle as origin of
coordinates. If Optimal reference point for holes into a face option is enabled,
referencing will be set for each hole indivi dually. If there are several holes on one
line and distance between them is the same (step), it is possible to shorten the pro-
gram and group the holes. For this purpose enable 
Group holes option.
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional through hole depth field
allows to take drill center point into account. Holes can be bored with a common drill
as well as with a cup borer type. Tool sele ction is specified with a hole diameter.
Hinge sinker “Cup” drill type if hole diameter exceeds field allows to set hole
diameter that will specify automatic tool selection in the program. Machine control
system group variants allow to select control system type. Set drill types option
allows to set automatic borer type and boring mode selection depending on hole di-
ameter and type. If it is enabled, drill table control elements will become available.

lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.

[Página 365]

10.443.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.

10.453.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Hole table allows to set tool automatic selection depending
on hole parameters. 
On drill shortest path and on diameter variants allow to set
holes boring order. For edge holes use Z and For holes on a face apply Z combo
boxes variants allow to choose a face for this parameter application. Tool lifting
between operations field allows to set tool height between operations.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.

10.460.  
If several holes are located on the same straight line and the distance between them

should be selected from a combo box.
Through holes can be drilled with drills for through as well as for blind holes. Drill
type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have center
point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed material
thickness. Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group allows to
take drill center point into account.

and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled. You can
map hole and mill diameter that will be automatically selected for its processing. For
this purpose push Diameter button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the

10.480.  
Append operations to CNC program group options allow to control operations
appending to control program. If there are several holes on one line and distance
between them is the same (step), it is possible to shorten the program and group
the holes. For this purpose enable 
Group holes option. Wood drills usually have
center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed
material thickness. 
Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group
allows to take drill center point into account.
It is necessary to set boring speed for blind and through holes and select borer type
for through holes. Saw blade width field allows to set saw blade width at linear
slots processing.

button allows to close the dialogue without changes.

gram and group the holes. For this purpose enable 
Group holes option.
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving

allows to take drill center point into account. Process linear horizontal slot option
allows to control slot processing parameters to the program appending.

[Página 401]
401
Chapter 10.
10.492.  
Sections control elements allow to select a tool, correction type, tool lead-in and
lead-out, etc. Execution of the whole contour option allows to set the elements
that will be milled. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled. If it is disabled, the
edges that do not coincide with overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is
rectangular and the option is disabled, contour will not be milled. 
Milling path di-
rection group variants allow to select tool direction.
Hole parameters
Holes processing is set in Hole section (fig. 10.493).
Fig. 10.492.

[Página 402]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
402
10.493.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option.
Through holes
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. It is necessary
to set boring speed for through holes in corresponding field and select working
mode.
Blind holes
It is necessary to set boring speed for blind holes in corresponding field and select
working mode.
Edge holes
It is necessary to set boring speed for edge holes in corresponding field.
Cup borer
Hinge sinker “Cup” drill type if hole diameter exceeds field allows to control
cup borer use. It is necessary to set bo ring speed for this borer in corresponding
field.
Linear slot
Linear slots parameters are set in Linear slot section (fig. 10.494).
Fig. 10.493.

tions speed and other parameters.

Milling parameters
Milling section control elements (fig. 10.502) allow to set milling parameters.
10.502.  
Tool combo box allows to select a milling tool name. Tools should be created be-
forehand in Tools section. Section control elements allow to set pass depth, pass
count, etc.
Execution of the whole contour option allows to set the elements for milling. If
it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled.  If it is disabled, the edges that do not
coincide with panel overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular,
Fig. 10.501.  
Fig. 10.502.

BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
410
and the option is disabled, contour will not be milled. Zigzag lead in option allows
to activate this operation. Group fields allow to set option parameters. Milling path
direction group variants allow to select tool moving direction.
Hole parameters
Holes processing is set in Hole section (fig. 10.503).
10.503.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. 
Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. If hole di-
ameter exceeds diameter of a mill, start from hole center option allows to
control holes with a mill processing order, from center or from perimeter.

the list.

group.

Allowance for part section control elements allow to set allowance values. Sec-

Contours parameters
Contours milling parameters are set in Outer contour (fig. 10.536) and Internal
contour sections of Process-dependent parameters tab.
10.536.  
Finish machining and Rough machining tabs control elements allow to select a
tool, correction types, tool lead-in and lead-out, etc. Rough machining option al-
lows to control this operation.
Control elements of these tabs are almost the same. There are additional control ele-
ments on Outer contour tab. Execution of the whole contour option allows to
set the elements that will be milled. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled. If
it is disabled, the edges that do not co incide with overall rectangle sides will be
milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular and the option is disabled, contour will not be
milled.
Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes parameters (fig. 10.537).
Fig. 10.536.

[Página 434]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
434
10.537.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option. Through holes can be bored with drills for through and blind
holes. Drill type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have
center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed
material thickness. 
Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group
allows to take drill center point into account. 
Tool numbers table allows to set automatic drill selection depending on hole type
and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled. You can
map hole and mill diameter that will be automatically selected for its processing. For
this purpose push Diameters button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the
screen (fig. 10.538).
10.538.  
Dialogue table contains holes diameter values and corresponding mill designations.
The fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add row button allows to add a blank row
to the table.
Fig. 10.537.  
Fig. 10.538.

[Página 435]
435
Chapter 10.
To delete the current row push Delete row button. Arrow buttons allow to change
current row position in the table. To finish setup push ОК button. Cancel button al-
lows to close the dialogue without changes. If a hole is processed with a mill, you
can select correction type from 
Correction combo box for particular mill diameter
set in Diameters field. For edge holes use Z group variants allow to select a face
for this parameter application.

alogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.540).
10.540.  
Slot name table contains all grooves names of the model. You can set particular
mills and a saw for each slot processing. Add mill button allows to add a tool to the
list. Mill name and its diameter can be edited in list fields. Correction type should be
selected from 
Correction combo box.

matically selected for its processing. For this purpose push 
Mill parameters but-
ton. Mill parameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.542).
