fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add button allows to add a blank row to the
table. To delete the current row push Delete button.

button allows to close the dialogue without changes.

Chapter 10.
10.601.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.

milled. If it is disabled, the edges that do  not coincide with panel overall rectangle

will not be milled. Path direction group variants allow to select tool moving direc-

Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes processing (fig. 10.612).
Fig. 10.611.

[Página 482]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
482
10.612.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. 
Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic spindle number selection
depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path and on diameter variants
allow to set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.

10.648.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. If there are
several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step), it is possi-
ble to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable 
Group
holes option. Control elements allow to set control programs creation parameters:
speed parameters, borer type, processed side, etc.
Processing using mill
Processing using mill (fig. 10.649) tab control elements allow to set holes milling
parameters.
Fig. 10.648.

[Página 510]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
510
10.649.  
If diameter is more or equal field allows to set hole diameter, if it exceeds, hole
will be milled. To map hole diameter  to a mill for its processing, push Diameters
button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the screen (fig. 10.650).
10.650.  
Dialogue table contains holes diameter values and corresponding mill designations.
The fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add row button allows to add a blank row
to the table.
To delete the current row push Delete row button. Arrow buttons allow to change
current row position in the table. To complete setup push ОК button. Cancel button
allows to close the dialogue without changes. «SCASSOC» command can be used
for through holes of great diameter milling. «TASCAC» program can be used for
blind holes of great diameter milling. 
SCASSOC for through hole and TASCAC
for blind holes groups control elements allow to set these commands use.

distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account.
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option.

10.679.  
Tool combo box allows to select a milling tool name created in Tools tab. Tools lists
should be created beforehand in Tools section. Execution of the whole contour
option allows to set the elements for milling. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be
milled. If it is disabled, the edges that do  not coincide with panel overall rectangle
sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rect angular, and the option is disabled, contour
will not be milled. Path direction group variants allow to select tool moving direc-
tion.
Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes processing (fig. 10.680).
Fig. 10.679.

[Página 530]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
530
10.680.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.

rameters.

10.695.  
If several holes are located on the same straight line and the distance between them
(step) is the same, it is possible to combine them and reduce the program volume.
For this purpose enabled Group holes using macro option. Macros files names
should be selected from a combo box.
Through holes can be drilled with drills for through as well as for blind holes. Drill
type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have center
point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed material
thickness. 
Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group allows to
take drill center point into account.

and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled.

cessing. For this purpose push Diameter button. Hole diameters dialogue will ap-
pear on the screen (fig. 10.696).

Contour processing parameters

10.714.  
Tool combo box allows to select a milling tool name from the list created in Tools

whole contour option allows to set the elements for milling. If it is enabled, all pan-
el edges will be milled. If it is disabled, the edges that do not coincide with panel
overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular, and the option is
disabled, contour will not be milled. Path direction group variants allow to select
tool moving direction.

[Página 555]
555
Chapter 10.
Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes processing (fig. 10.715).
10.715.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindle to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. 
Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic
spindle number selection depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path
and on diameter variants allow to set holes boring order.

10.725.  
Tool combo box allows to select a milling tool name. Tools lists should be created
beforehand in Tools section. Section control elements allow to set pass depth, pass
count, etc. Execution of the whole contour option allows to set the elements for
milling. If it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled. If it is disabled, the edges that
do not coincide with panel overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rect-
angular, and the option is disabled, contour will not be milled.

Hole parameters
Hole section control elements allow to set holes processing (fig. 10.726).
Fig. 10.725.

[Página 562]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
562
10.726.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance. 
Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic spindle number selection
depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path and on diameter variants
allow to set holes boring order.
If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point located on the diameter of the
hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill enters the material at the point
of the hole center, then there will be no such defects. Through hole milling from
the center option allows to control the position of the hole start point: in the center
or on the diameter of the hole.

Chapter 10.
10.733.  
If there are several holes on one line and distance between them is the same (step),
it is possible to shorten the program and group the holes. For this purpose enable
Group holes option. Through holes can be bored with drills for through and blind
holes. Drill type group variants allow to select a tool type. Wood drills usually have
center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving distance should exceed
material thickness. Additional depth field in Through hole parameters group
allows to take drill center point into account. 
Tool numbers table allows to set automatic drill selection depending on hole type
and diameter. This setting will work if Set tool numbers option is enabled. You can
map hole and mill diameter that will be automatically selected for its processing. For
this purpose push Diameters button. Hole diameters dialogue will appear on the
screen (fig. 10.734).
10.734.  
Dialogue table contains holes diameter values and corresponding mill designations.
The fields should be filled via a keyboard. Add row button allows to add a blank row
to the table.

Fig. 10.734.

Correction combo box for particular mill diameter

select To offset point (X, dY) or To point with specified coordinates variants,
return point coordinates are specified by values of corresponding fields. Tool lift-
ing between operations field allows to set tool height.
To process the panel, two separate files can be created containing programs for the
front and non-front sides. Do not mill outer and internal contours for non-
front side processing file if there is a file for front side option allows to control
milling program in non-front side file.
Usable commands
Commands section control elements (fig. 10.768) allow to control separate com-
mands to control program inclusion.

Chapter 10.
Contour processing parameters
Outer and internal contours processing parameters are set in  Milling section
(fig. 10.772).
10.772.  
Tool combo box allows to select a tool for milling from a list on Tools tab. Execu-
tion of the whole contour option allows to set the elements that will be milled. If
it is enabled, all panel edges will be milled.  If it is disabled, the edges that do not
coincide with overall rectangle sides will be milled. I.e. if panel is rectangular and the
option is disabled, contour will not be milled. Path direction of internal and external
contour milling group variants allow to select tool direction.
Hole parameters
Holes processing is set in Hole section (fig. 10.773).
Fig. 10.772.

[Página 592]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
592
10.773.  
Wood drills usually have center point. Thus, for through holes boring, drill moving
distance should exceed material thickness. Additional depth field in Through
hole parameters group allows to take drill center point into account. Before edge
hole boring it is necessary to move a spindl e to material panel in a way, that it will
not collide with it. Safety distance parameter in Edge hole parameters group al-
lows to set the distance.
Drill holes on a face group table allows to set automatic spindle number selection
depending on hole parameters. On drill shortest path and on diameter variants
allow to set holes boring order. If, when drilling through holes, the mill enters a point
located on the diameter of the hole, it can pull out material at the outlet. If the mill
enters the material at the point of the hole center, then there will be no such defects.
Through hole milling from the center option allows to control the position of the
hole start point: in the center or on the diameter of the hole.
Slots processing parameters
Grooving section control elements (fig. 10.774)  allow to set slots processing pa-
rameters.
Fig. 10.773.

and linear slots of X and Y axes processing. Tool lists should be created beforehand
in

ing. After pushing the button Tool choice dialogue will appear on the screen
(fig. 10.775).
10.775.  
Slot name table contains all slots names in the model. You can assign particular
saws and mills for each slot processing. Add mill button allows to assign a tool for
slot processing from the list created in section Tools parameters on p. 589.
Delete button allows to delete selected tool from the list. To complete tool assign-
ment push ОК button. Cancel button allows to close the dialogue without changes.

Chapter 11.Control program files creation
BAZIS-CNC module work result is control programs generation and their files sav-
ing. For this purpose do the following:
▼ set control programs files creation order (see Chapter 9 on p. 51),
▼ set process-dependent parameters (see Chapter 10 on p. 63),
▼ choose panels for processing programs generation, check in panel line in Preview
for CNC window table (see section 8.1 on p. 44),
▼ push Export button in the window (fig. 7.1 on p. 41).
A progress bar will appear on the screen.
After export completion, a message will appear on the screen (fig. 11.1).
11.1.  
If no panel is selected, export will not be fulfilled and a warning will appear on the
screen (fig. 11.2).
11.2.  
In this case select the panels and try to export again. To complete control program
files creation push Close button. Preview for CNC window will be closed.
If there are few panels, progress bar may not appear.
Fig. 11.1.  
Fig. 11.2.

[Página 599]
599
Appendix I.Terms and definitions
Active document
It is the current document you work wi th. If documents windows are maximized,
document file name is in the module title bar.
If document windows are not maximized, active window title bar is highlighted.
Active window
It is the window where active document is opened.
Material DB
It is a structured list of materials, tools, products used for furniture products models
creation.
Material DB creation, editing and use is described in detail in BAZIS-Woodworker
User Guide.
Operation DB
Operation DB is a structured list of operations, executed with materials for furniture
models creation or with completed products . It is used for automatic product cost
calculation.
DB creation, editing and use is described in BAZIS-Estimate module documentation.
Cost items DB
Cost items DB is a structured list of estimate items for calculation.
It is used for automatic product cost calculation.
DB creation, editing and use is described in detail in BAZIS-Estimate module docu-
mentation.
Object base point
It is a point in the model window or on a drawing sheet for 3D and 2D model objects
position determination. It is used at clipboard use, model or fragment from a file in-
sertion, at objects work, e.g. Move, Rotate, Copy by points, Copy by line, Array
to grid, at fragment to library adding and selected objects as fragment saving.
Any window or sheet point can be selected as a base point, it is reasonable to select
an object typical point. E.g. if the model is a sphere, select its center or a point on
its surface as a base point. If drawing object is a rectangle, select its vertex, center,
side middle, etc. as a base point. This makes the work with models more conve-
nient.

[Página 600]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
600
If you move the cursor, the object will be snapped to it with a base point. For accu-
rate positioning use snaps.
If parameters of object in a window position are set in Property manager fields,
these values also relate to a base point.
Panel base point
It is one of panel typical points, snapped to a cursor at current construction stage.
It is used at panel installation.
If you move the cursor in the window, a base point and a model will follow it. If you
push mouse left button the model will be fi xed in a way, that its base point will be
placed in a point of cursor position. For accurate model positioning use snaps.
Panel parallelepiped vertexes and its edges middle points can be used as base
points. To change the panel base point position push < Space> and <Tab> buttons.
Diagonal cross stands for a base point.
Balustrade
Balustrade is a fencing (usually short) of a stairway, balcony, terrace, etc. composed
of a set of shaped columns (balusters) joined by a handrail or a horizontal beam at
the top; handrail made of shaped columns.
BAZIS system library
A library is a file, where several documents of different types of BAZIS system are
saved – sheets, fragments. The libraries can be used for storing the documents, re-
lated to a certain furniture item.
By default, bln extension is assigned to library files.
Label
Label is a sheet of paper (self-adhesive) of custom dimension which is pasted to a
panel or a cut-off after cutout completion. Labels are used for unique panels identi-
fication and for fast searching, processing and assembly. For work with labels auto-
mation, information on them can be generated in a bar code form. In this case, scan-
ners can be used for labels reading.
Block
It is a named group of drawing objects. It is reasonable to add functionally finished
image to a block, e.g. view, layer, drawer, etc.
Objects, included to the block are selected  as a single unit. Separate elements can
be edited in a block. Blocks may contain other blocks.
Blocks are described in detail in BAZIS-Woodworker module documentation.

[Página 601]
601
Terms and definitions
View
It is a part of a drawing that contains ge ometrical objects. It has a local coordinate
system and viewing scale that may differ from drawing common settings.
Views are described in detail in BAZIS-Woodworker module documentation.
Material group
It is a structural element of Material DB. It contains material list united by function,
e.g. Sheet material, Linear material, Tools.
Operation group
Operation group is a structural unit of operation DB . It contains a list of operations
of similar functionality, e.g. Operations with material, Operations with a ready prod-
uct.
Cost item group
Cost item group is a structural unit of cost items DB. It contains cost items list to be
calculated, e.g. Costs, Markup.
Part
Parts are panels obtained from additional panel list cutting.
Cutting job
Cutting job is a panel list of current material.
Snap keys
For snap execution place a cursor about a snap point  and push a corresponding
snap key. Keys can be assigned according to snap types. By default the following
buttons correspond to snaps:
▼ <Alt>  – the nearest point of the nearest element.
▼ <Ctrl> or mouse middle button – element origin or end point, arc or circle center,
two objects intersection point. 
▼ <F8>  – object middle.
Material class
Class is a set of materials united on the basis of some peculiarity. Common material
class is always present in data base. Optional classes can be created by the user.
Each material is included into common class irrespective of additional classes it
may relate to.

[Página 602]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
602
Materials are grouped into classes in BA ZIS-Estimate module at datasets creation
and for price formation rules.
Class examples:
▼ main materials,
▼ auxiliary materials,
▼ tool degradation.
Operation class
Operation class is a set of operations united by a certain criterion. Common opera-
tion class is always present in the DB. Additional classes can be created if required.
The operations are grouped into classes in BAZIS-Estimate module when the output
documents are generated or when the pricing rules are set.
Calculation item class
Calculation item class is a set of calculation items united by a certain criterion. Com-
mon item class is always present in the database. Additional classes can be created
if required.
The calculation items are grouped into classes in BAZIS-Estimate module when the
output documents are generated or when the pricing rules are set.
Material excess factor
Material excess factor is an empiric factor for material amount calculation for mak-
ing extra products with respect of waste and rejects.
Series factor
Series factor allows to take into accoun t the reduced work content due to identical
items in an order. This factor value is set on the basis of total operation timing.
Panel face and back sides
Sides notion is used at model drawings automatic creation. Face side is a panel face
which is the most convenient for viewing considering slots and holes on both panel
faces.
Face panels are those, that are placed in front view in the following ways:
▼ horizontal – above,
▼ vertical – from the right,
▼ front – from the front.
Scale
It is object linear dimensions on a drawing to object actual dimensions ratio.

[Página 603]
603
Terms and definitions
Scaling is used for objects image on a drawing sheet arrangement in a case, if object
dimensions exceed sheet dimensions or for small object scaled-up viewing.
Image scales should be selected according to GOST 2.302-68.
Several views may be arranged on a drawing sheet each on different scales.
Auxiliary materials
Auxiliary materials are materials which are not directly included in the output prod-
uct, but were used in production process.
Auxiliary materials fall into eight groups depending on their purpose (tab. 1).
The required amount of auxiliary materials is usually calculated on concomitance.
Main materials
Main materials are materials constituting the main components of the final product.
Main materials are distinguished as separate element in planning and cost account-
ing.
Decreasing scales 1:2; 1:2,5; 1:4; 1:5; 1:10; 1:15; 1:20; 1:25; 1:40; 1:50; 
1:75; 1:100; 1:200; 1:400; 1:500; 1:800; 1:1000
Natural scale 1:1
Increasing scales 2:1; 2,5:1; 4:1; 5:1; 10:1; 20:1; 40:1; 50:1; 100:1
Tab. 1.  Auxiliary materials groups
Group number Material purpose Example
1 Impart defined characteristics to a ready 
product.
Varnishes, paint
2 Direct influence on main materials . Mills, saws, drills.
3 Equipment and rooms maintenance. Lubricants, rags.
4 Equipment and rooms repair.
5 Staff support. Working clothes.
6 In-plant logistics.
7 Procurement and sales. Packing materials.
8 Management support. Stationery.

[Página 604]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
604
Model parameter material
Model parameter material is material, the amount and cost of which are calculated
on the basis of geometrical dimensions of furniture product made of this material.
Material cost in terms of a model parameter is included into the total product cost.
Material by parameter from the model
The material, the amount of which is calculated for calculating the cost, based on
the geometric dimensions of the element of a furniture product made of this mate-
rial. Material cost according to the parameter from the model is included in the total
cost of the product.
Cut-off
Cut-off is a board fragment from a previous cutout which can be used in further cut-
out.
Depending on its dimensions, a fragment can be considered as waste  or a cut-off.
The fragment dimensions for waste or cu t-offs purpose are set at BAZIS-Cutting
module setup.
Model parameter operation
This operation is obligatory for processing a certain material or a group of materials
at operations with materials. Cost of atte ndant operations is included into the total
product cost.
The number of operations, e.g. boring or rectangle cut length, is the same as the pa-
rameter of the model analysis.
Example of concomitance in terms of a model parameter.
For all materials in Particleboard group, Drilling blind holes in a face  operation is
concomitant in terms of a model parameter. At the model analysis, Blind hole count
parameter of the model is calculated and then operation cost is multiplied by this
value.
See also Concomitant operation
.
For operations with products – the number of assembly operations performed, e.g.
joining with different fasteners, mounting face fasteners, etc.
Orthogonal constructions
In most cases, you can move the cursor at any angle to coordinate axes.
Orthogonal construction mode helps limit the cursor movements to directions, par-
allel to coordinate axes. However, the actual cursor movements can be not only or-
thogonal. The cursor trajectory will be parallel to the coordinate axis, if the angle be-

[Página 605]
605
Terms and definitions
tween the origin point and the current cursor position does not exceed the value
specified at system settings (fig. 11.3), see BAZIS System setup User Guide.
11.3.  
Waste
Waste is a board fragment from a previous cutout, which cannot be used in further
cutout.
Depending on its dimensions, one and th e same fragment can be considered as
waste or a cut-off. The fragment dimensions for waste or cut-offs distribution are
set at BAZIS-Cutting module setup.
Property manager
Property manager control elements allow to control geometrical constructions
commands. X, Y, Z fields contain the cursor current position coordinate values in
the model window. Other control elements content depend on the command and ex-
amined at the command description.
Zooming
It is geometrical objects visual dimensions changing in document window. It is used
with the documents of different types. It is not connected with object image scaling
.
Parametrical modeling
Parametrical modeling allows to use models which description contains parame-
ters, e.g. geometrical dimensions. Parameter editing leads to corresponding model
shape or its position in space changing. Parametrical modeling allows to create ar-
bitrary model copies number that differ in parameters from one another.
Fig. 11.3.

[Página 606]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
606
Purchase part
A named group of furniture product model objects. It is advisable to include a func-
tionally complete image in the composition of the purchased part, e.g. a cabinet, a
door, etc. Regardless of the composition of the purchased part, that is, the number
and size of the panels it consists of, its cost in BAZIS-Estimate module is taken into
account in pieces. The panels that make up the purchased part are not transferred
to the BAZIS-Cutting module. The objects included in the purchased part are high-
lighted as a single whole.
Semiproducts
It is a named object group. Depending on module settings it can be accounted as
assemblies
 or a block.
Crosscut-mitre fence DIGIT L
Crosscut-mitre fence DIGIT L helps set length accurate within 0.1 mm. The system
displays the dimensions of all flip stops. All dimensions calibration is executed on
an integrated central measuring-indicated control unit.
Snap
Snap is cursor moving into the object snap point in the document window.
This function is used for exact constructions. It is executed by pushing hotkeys.
Precut
Precut is a cut at stack cutting, executed for sheet edges alignment in the pack. This
ensures the panel dimensions equality in a pack.
Point
The programs of computer makeup use the point proposed by Adobe which is equal
to 1/72 inch, i.e. 25.4/72 mm = 0.3527 mm.
Concomitant operation
It is an operation which realization is obligatory with set material, material group or
operation. Accompanying operations cost is included into product total cost.
Accompanying operation execution number corresponds to main material amount
or operation work content.
Here is the following example of material concomitance.
At each fastener Roller slider 450  mounting, Drawer sliders operation  is executed
once.
See Concomitant operation of a model parameter.

[Página 607]
607
Terms and definitions
Concomitant material
Concomitant material is auxiliary material which is compulsory for use with the main
material/material group or for operation execution. The amount of concomitant ma-
terial depends on the main material or operation and corresponds to the main ma-
terial amount or operation work content. The cost of concomitant materials is in-
cluded into the total product cost.
An example of concomitance is the following. At any material from Particleboard
group use, Rag material is used automatically in the proportion of 0.003 kg per 1 sq.
m of panel.
Compound parameter
Compound parameter is a cutout parameter which can be displayed on a label. This
is a combination of several standard cutout  parameters. It is used in BAZIS-Label
module.
Tariff rate
Tariff rate defines the standard hour cost depending on the complexity of the current
operation and employee qualification, required for it. It is used for automatic opera-
tions cost calculation.
Material type
The following types of materials and material groups can be set:
▼ Other materials,
▼ Sheet and linear materials,
▼ Edge banding material,
▼ Fasteners and accessories.
Material types are system, they can not be customized. Materials division into types
makes usable material table editing convenient.
Cursor snap point
The following objects can be used as snap points:
▼ parallelepiped vertex,
▼ parallelepiped edge middle,
▼ object origin or end point, e.g. an arc or a line segment.
▼ two objects intersection point,
▼ arc or circle center,
▼ the nearest point of nearest object,
▼ object middle.

[Página 608]
BAZIS-CNC. User Guide
608
Material types
Material group to some type relation ensures group viewing in panel material table
or edge banding dialogues.
Phantom
Phantom is an image that appears on the screen at some operation execution and it
shows the current state of created or edited objects.
If you change cursor position or object parameters, the phantom will be rebuilt dy-
namically in Property manager field and it shows object new state.
Fragment
It is a 2D or 3D image with a set base point, saved to a file with frw extension.
It is reasonable to save typical and freque ntly used objects as fragments, e.g. hole
symbolic representations on drawings, typical 3D objects, etc.
Accessories
Accessories are divided in BAZIS system in to fasteners and functional or face ac-
cessories.
Fasteners are used for immovable (pins, screws, L-brackets, connectors, shelfhold-
ers) and movable (hinges, sliders) furniture elements fixing.
Functional accessories ensure furniture and its elements functionality. They include
handles, legs, locks and latches, etc. In this case built-in domestic appliances ele-
ments belong to accessories – hobs, ovens, sinks, etc.
Accessory for assignment by parameter
This parameter is used automated accessory cost calculation at BAZIS-Estimate
module.
This group accessory parameter is calculated at the model analysis. E.g. for confir-
mat screws of all nominal sizes Confirmat screws material amount parameter will
be calculated. Confirmat screw cost is multiplied by obtained score. All accessories
cost will be calculated automatically in the same way.
Parametrical accessories
Parametrical accessory is a holes set math ematical model, that ensures automatic
accessory referencing. Parameters changing leads to corresponding model shape
change.
One and the same parametrical accessory can be installed by different panel con-
nection types. Required type is specified automatically at the panels for connection
pointing.

[Página 609]
609
Terms and definitions
Parametrical accessories data are stored in Fasteners.config.
Arbitrary accessories
Arbitrary accessory may include the following components:
▼ holes parametrical models, that specify model referencing mode on the panel face.
▼ 3D model, that ensures product visualization.
Each arbitrary accessory can be installe d only according to panel connection type
its model has been designed for.
Each model of arbitrary accessory is saved in a file with f3d extension.
Stacking
Stacking in BAZIS-Cutting module is panels layout on cutting charts when panels
will be finished according to set parameters.
Sketch
It is a flat geometrical figure that is a base for geometrical bodies construction.
It consists of one or several (for extruded boss) contours. Sketch contours meet the
following demands:
▼ all sketch contours should be closed,
▼ if sketch has several contours, they should not intersect or touch each other.
